`SHA256=46655a7d7c998dbb65f070271f6962422e66df6d32976f95558d8d2e55696d21`

```yaml
schema_version: forprint_human_intent_delta_v0_1
date: '2026-08-31'
status: approved_evening_review_delta
authority: append_to_canonical_module_ledgers
modules:
  forprint_system_blueprint:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Expanded portfolio rendering/content rules must be documented so the same recognizable PDF structure
        can be regenerated by another assistant or generator.
      context: Portfolio layout must not depend on conversational memory.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Portfolio roadmap should show the rough full route to target state even when distant steps are not yet
        fully discussed.
      context: Future steps may be synthetic if they are visibly marked and do not pretend to be approved implementation.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Synthetic portfolio content is useful and should remain visible with a distinct status/color plus confidence/evidence
        basis.
      context: Synthetic does not mean bad; it means useful forward projection that still needs review.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-017
      status: AGREED
      text: Historical portfolio PDFs must remain in the project under versioned/date filenames and must not be
        overwritten by a newer projection.
      context: The PDF is history/projection, not release authority.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-04
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-018
      status: AGREED
      text: Cross-module semantic invariants should become a machine-readable governance surface consumed by Inspector.
      context: Examples include phone-not-identity, QR-not-permission and filename-not-source-of-truth.
      roadmap: BP-SEMANTIC-01
  forprint_project_inspector:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Project Inspector owns Semantic Consistency Audit as an internal capability; no separate semantic-audit
        ForPrint module is created.
      context: Keep the ecosystem small; Inspector remains independent and does not become domain owner.
      roadmap: PI-S1
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-008
      status: AGREED
      text: Incremental Inspector review must compare last_inspected_commit/checkpoint to the current target commit,
        not rely only on git status.
      context: Committed daytime changes must remain visible to the next audit.
      roadmap: PI-S1
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-009
      status: AGREED
      text: Anything that can be proven deterministically should be checked by scripts before involving an LLM.
      context: Schemas, revisions, indexes, tests, contract support and evidence are cheaper/more reliable mechanically.
      roadmap: PI-S2
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-010
      status: AGREED
      text: When interpretive policy/business/legal/customer-facing content changes, Inspector builds a bounded
        semantic audit package for an LLM worker.
      context: The worker receives changed content plus applicable authorities, contracts, Human Intent and explicit
        questions.
      roadmap: PI-S4
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-011
      status: AGREED
      text: The semantic LLM worker may ask Inspector for additional bounded context instead of guessing missing
        authority.
      context: Inspector can repack relevant documents and rerun the review.
      roadmap: PI-S5
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Semantic review triggers are risk-based, not diff-size-based; a one-line policy change can require immediate
        LLM review.
      context: High-risk categories include identity, payment, legal, customer-facing, production-safety and authority
        changes.
      roadmap: PI-S7
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-013
      status: AGREED
      text: A portfolio-wide semantic sweep should initially run about monthly and may move to quarterly/semiannual
        cadence as the architecture matures.
      context: Event-driven high-risk audits remain active even when full-sweep cadence decreases.
      roadmap: PI-S8
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Inspector semantic findings need a stable taxonomy covering contradiction, ambiguity, ownership violation,
        contract divergence, term drift, stale authority, Human Intent divergence, hidden defaults, orphan rules
        and cross-module behavior conflict.
      context: Findings must be structured rather than a vague AI opinion.
      roadmap: PI-S6
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Semantic findings must carry severity, confidence, evidence, affected modules and recommended resolution
        owner.
      context: The report must be usable by Blueprint/human governance.
      roadmap: PI-S6
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Inspector and its LLM worker detect, verify, compare, classify, report and route; they do not invent
        business rules or silently rewrite domain semantics.
      context: A suspected defect in an authority document is routed back to that authority owner.
      roadmap: PI-S10
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-017
      status: AGREED
      text: For legally meaningful content, LLM review performs issue spotting and contradiction analysis but does
        not declare material legal compliance confirmed.
      context: Final legal certainty remains a separate human/legal-review boundary.
      roadmap: PI-S10
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-018
      status: AGREED
      text: Inspector should consume a future machine-readable Cross-Module Invariant Registry maintained under
        Blueprint governance.
      context: The registry makes semantic checks more deterministic without giving Inspector semantic ownership.
      roadmap: PI-S3
  forprint_contract_registry:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-006
      status: AGREED
      text: 'The previous open Contract Registry lifecycle question is conceptually resolved: the module is a versioned
        registry of significant inter-module interface agreements.'
      context: This supersedes the uncertainty expressed by HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-005 without deleting that historical
        GAP.
      roadmap: CR-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Library owns reusable domain semantics; Contract Registry owns the versioned interface agreement that
        carries those semantics between modules.
      context: Contract Registry must not become Library.
      roadmap: CR-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-008
      status: AGREED
      text: Gateway is a runtime consumer/enforcer of ACTIVE contracts; it is not the canonical home of contract
        lifecycle.
      context: Contract Registry and Gateway remain separate responsibilities.
      roadmap: CR-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-009
      status: AGREED
      text: A contract record should track provider, consumers, revision, purpose, schemas/artifacts, semantic authority
        references, compatibility, validators, examples, adoption and migration lifecycle.
      context: Exact physical schema is implementation work, but this target information set is agreed.
      roadmap: CR-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-010
      status: AGREED
      text: Contract lifecycle should distinguish DRAFT, PROPOSED, READY_FOR_ADOPTION, ACTIVE, SUPPORTED_LEGACY,
        DEPRECATED, BLOCKED, REVOKED and RETIRED.
      context: Lifecycle details can be refined while preserving the distinction between candidate, active, legacy
        and unsafe revisions.
      roadmap: CR-04
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-011
      status: AGREED
      text: Contract Registry must classify breaking versus non-breaking revisions instead of assuming compatibility
        from a version string.
      context: A breaking revision cannot silently become active while required consumers are unprepared.
      roadmap: CR-05
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Each contract revision needs a provider/consumer adoption matrix and independent Inspector conformance
        evidence.
      context: Blueprint uses the matrix to prevent one module running ahead of dependencies.
      roadmap: CR-06
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-013
      status: AGREED
      text: Contracts should carry representative fixtures and edge-case test vectors so support can be proven,
        not merely declared in documentation.
      context: Inspector can execute common conformance suites.
      roadmap: CR-07
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Contract changes need impact analysis showing direct and important indirect affected modules.
      context: This feeds Blueprint dependency balancing and rollout planning.
      roadmap: CR-08
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Core interface meaning should remain transport-neutral where practical; HTTP/queue/internal-call adapters
        should not silently redefine business semantics.
      context: Transport belongs to runtime integration concerns.
      roadmap: CR-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Contract Registry records readiness/adoption but does not unilaterally activate breaking ecosystem semantics.
      context: At current maturity, Blueprint remains the activation governance point after required evidence.
      roadmap: CR-12
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-017
      status: AGREED
      text: The first full Contract Registry lifecycle pilot will be the Calculator to Operations Control Registry
        Job Specification contract.
      context: The Calculator target is not fully fixed, so exact pilot fields remain future detailed work.
      roadmap: CR-09
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CONTRACT-REGISTRY-018
      status: AGREED
      text: The mature target includes safe deprecation/migration/retirement and a portfolio compatibility view,
        but late automation steps remain synthetic until proven by earlier pilots.
      context: Keep the full horizon visible without pretending late mechanics are already decided.
      roadmap: CR-14
  forprint_integration_gateway:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-006
      status: AGREED
      text: Gateway should validate and route runtime payloads against published ACTIVE contract revisions rather
        than owning the contract lifecycle itself.
      context: Contract Registry says what is agreed; Gateway applies that agreement in transport/runtime.
      roadmap: GW-CONTRACT-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Gateway must not silently reinterpret Library/CRM/Accounting semantics when normalizing a payload.
      context: If a semantic mapping changes, it requires the appropriate authority/contract revision.
      roadmap: GW-CONTRACT-02
  forprint_library:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-009
      status: AGREED
      text: Library owns canonical aliases, common misspellings, short production tokens, naming profiles and profile-specific
        defaults for materials/operations/device capabilities.
      context: Calculator consumes these definitions instead of maintaining a private vocabulary.
      roadmap: LIB-FILE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-010
      status: AGREED
      text: A short human filename may omit common information only when the active naming profile defines the default
        unambiguously.
      context: 'Example: Ricoh lamMat represents the agreed default thin matte roll lamination.'
      roadmap: LIB-FILE-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-011
      status: AGREED
      text: The same short token may have different meaning across equipment/production profiles, so structured
        profile/capability context must remain explicit.
      context: Do not globally interpret a token without its naming/production profile.
      roadmap: LIB-FILE-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Library semantic changes that affect inter-module payloads must flow through a versioned Contract Registry/adoption
        process rather than being changed locally in one consumer.
      context: Avoid modules drifting onto different meanings of the same field/token.
      roadmap: LIB-FILE-04
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-013
      status: AGREED
      text: Library should eventually provide standardized visual/SOP instructions for Job Ticket and Operations
        Assistant steps, with manager-provided notes/images as fallback when no standard exists.
      context: Human guidance is a Library knowledge responsibility; execution truth remains OCR.
      roadmap: LIB-FILE-05
  calculator_engine:
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator is the primary implementation owner for parsing, normalizing and rendering human production
        filenames, while Library owns the vocabulary/profile semantics.
      context: One generator/parser should prevent different modules inventing different naming rules.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-01
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Canonical human production filenames use single underscore semantic blocks; mandatory double-underscore
        separators are rejected as visually unnecessary.
      context: Human typing speed and readability are first-class requirements.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-02
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-016
      status: AGREED
      text: In the production filename quantity expression copies x N, N means physical sheets per copy, not PDF
        pages.
      context: The operator thinks in sheets; future validation must preserve this meaning.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-03
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-017
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator should validate manually created filenames against deterministic file/job evidence and correct/report
        sheet-count mismatches when the production convention makes the result unambiguous.
      context: If the evidence is ambiguous, ask rather than silently guess.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-04
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-018
      status: AGREED
      text: For the Ricoh sheet profile, lamMat is the short human token for the agreed default matte 25 µm roll
        lamination without forcing the operator to type 25.
      context: Canonical database state still stores the exact lamination properties.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-05
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-019
      status: AGREED
      text: 'Manual/offline production files remain supported: after systems recover, Calculator parses and reconciles
        them into canonical job/order flow while preserving original filename and provenance.'
      context: Do not make server availability a requirement for emergency manual production.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-06
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-020
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator should emit required production capability/profile context in structured data so an abstract
        file is not misinterpreted when removed from its legacy machine directory.
      context: Human filename does not need to carry a physical printer serial number.
      roadmap: CALC-FILE-07
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-021
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator Job Specification is the agreed first Contract Registry lifecycle pilot toward Operations
        Control Registry.
      context: The exact contract remains to be detailed when Calculator target architecture is sufficiently mature.
      roadmap: CALC-CONTRACT-01
  forprint_crm:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-009
      status: AGREED
      text: One immutable person may simultaneously have a personal customer context and active representation of
        one or more organizations.
      context: A phone number identifies the person/contact path, not a single billing customer.
      roadmap: CRM-ID-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-010
      status: AGREED
      text: Organization representation remains temporal; when a person leaves an organization the relation closes
        for new orders while historical order snapshots remain intact.
      context: Do not let former employee orders fall onto the old organization.
      roadmap: CRM-ID-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-011
      status: AGREED
      text: An order must record the selected billing/customer context separately from the person who is communicating.
      context: The same person can order notebooks personally and company materials for Medicom using one phone
        number.
      roadmap: CRM-ID-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-012
      status: AGREED
      text: CRM should preserve normalized phone identity plus human display formatting and fast last-four lookup
        without using the phone as database primary key.
      context: Legacy folders/filenames may still show spaced phone formatting for operators.
      roadmap: CRM-ID-04
  telegram_bot:
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-008
      status: AGREED
      text: When a person can represent an organization, Telegram should lightly state the currently selected customer/billing
        context once per relevant order/dialogue.
      context: 'Example: ''оформляю замовлення на Medicom'' so the person sees where the order will be billed.'
      roadmap: TG-ID-01
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-009
      status: AGREED
      text: Telegram must let the same person switch an order from organization context to personal context without
        creating a duplicate person.
      context: One channel identity may legitimately map to several business contexts.
      roadmap: TG-ID-02
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-010
      status: AGREED
      text: Closed organization representations must not be proposed for new Telegram orders, while historical order
        snapshots remain reconstructible.
      context: Temporal CRM relationship drives channel suggestions.
      roadmap: TG-ID-03
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-011
      status: AGREED
      text: Order-relevant Telegram confirmations should be persisted as structured/auditable evidence linked to
        the order, independent of whether the channel UI later still shows the message.
      context: A future retention/privacy policy must govern how much raw conversation is retained.
      roadmap: TG-AUDIT-01
  forprint_operations_control_registry:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-011
      status: AGREED
      text: Operations Control Registry owns the versioned Job Ticket as the complete human production document;
        filename remains a compact projection.
      context: Job Ticket can carry detailed operations, geometry, priority, holds, QR and revision.
      roadmap: OCR-TICKET-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-012
      status: AGREED
      text: The future order/job identifier replaces the legacy dollar marker as the structured link between production
        file and canonical order/accounting flow.
      context: Dollar remains a legacy hint only.
      roadmap: OCR-FILE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-013
      status: AGREED
      text: Legacy wait should become explicit HOLD reasons such as HOLD_APPROVAL, HOLD_PAYMENT or HOLD_CLARIFICATION
        with START blocked until the gate clears.
      context: The file may be prepared in advance but cannot start printing while held.
      roadmap: OCR-HOLD-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Legacy first should become structured expedite/priority class rather than hidden filename ordering.
      context: Several jobs may share the same high-priority class.
      roadmap: OCR-PRIORITY-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Reprint must be a linked child-job workflow with reason, responsibility, quantity/material impact and
        separate billing consequence.
      context: Internal defect reprint may cost ForPrint without charging the customer.
      roadmap: OCR-REPRINT-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Proof/sample purpose must be structured independently from billing policy; a prova/proof can be free,
        included or paid.
      context: Production purpose must not silently dictate accounting.
      roadmap: OCR-PROOF-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-017
      status: AGREED
      text: A scanned stale Job Ticket/QR must warn that the paper revision is old and offer the current ticket;
        QR identifies the job but is not permission to execute.
      context: Current order state remains the execution gate.
      roadmap: OCR-TICKET-02
  forprint_operations_assistant:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Operations Assistant should teach a new worker how to read the short production filename and which material/process
        each token implies in the active profile.
      context: Use text first, then schemes/reference images, later real equipment photos when useful.
      roadmap: OA-TICKET-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-013
      status: AGREED
      text: Operations Assistant displays the current Job Ticket on phone/tablet and can request another paper copy
        if the original is lost or damaged.
      context: OCR owns the ticket revision; Assistant is the human interface.
      roadmap: OA-TICKET-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Assistant production queue should expose structured priority and HOLD state with text/icon as well as
        color; color alone is insufficient.
      context: Urgent jobs must be obvious and held jobs must not look runnable.
      roadmap: OA-QUEUE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Where Library has standardized finishing geometry, Assistant can show a visual final-result hint; otherwise
        an order-specific manager note/image can be displayed.
      context: 'Example: hole diameter and top-center position on a badge/tag.'
      roadmap: OA-TICKET-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Assistant should expose a simple reprint reporting action from the Job Ticket/QR flow so the worker
        can report defect/reprint reason without learning a complex ERP.
      context: The resulting reprint semantics belong to OCR/Accounting/Warehouse.
      roadmap: OA-REPRINT-01
  forprint_prepress_hub:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PREPRESS-HUB-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Prepress should expose a reusable deterministic document probe for PDF page count, dimensions and other
        technical evidence used by Calculator validation.
      context: Calculator initiates business validation; Prepress supplies file facts.
      roadmap: PP-FILE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PREPRESS-HUB-008
      status: AGREED
      text: Production filename sheet-count correction may be automatic only when the agreed production/PDF convention
        makes physical sheet count deterministic; otherwise clarification is required.
      context: Do not guess duplex/imposition meaning from ambiguous files.
      roadmap: PP-FILE-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PREPRESS-HUB-009
      status: AGREED
      text: PDF production metadata may later carry stable production properties as an interoperability projection,
        but it does not replace canonical order/job state.
      context: JDF/XJDF/PDF metadata remain future adapter directions.
      roadmap: PP-INTEROP-01
  forprint_system_administration:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-ADMINISTRATION-006
      status: AGREED
      text: System Administration must distinguish physical device instances by stable asset/device identity, model
        and serial even when workers casually call several machines 'Epson'.
      context: Operational analytics must know which actual device performed work.
      roadmap: SYS-DEVICE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-ADMINISTRATION-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Production planning should distinguish required capability, eligible device group/queue and concrete
        device instance instead of encoding a printer serial in every human filename.
      context: This permits failover between similar machines.
      roadmap: SYS-DEVICE-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-ADMINISTRATION-008
      status: AGREED
      text: System Administration owns physical routing/fallback for Job Ticket printing; Operations Assistant may
        request a reprint but does not own printer configuration.
      context: Keep human interface separate from infrastructure routing.
      roadmap: SYS-TICKET-01
  production_runtime_inspector:
    - intent_id: HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-006
      status: AGREED
      text: Runtime Inspector records the concrete physical device instance actually used for an operation, not
        only a generic machine family.
      context: Needed for maintenance, quality and true machine-performance analytics.
      roadmap: PRI-DEVICE-01
    - intent_id: HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-007
      status: AGREED
      text: Similar/replaceable printers may share a capability queue while runtime evidence still identifies the
        exact device used.
      context: Planning capability and actual telemetry are separate concerns.
      roadmap: PRI-DEVICE-02
  forprint_accounting_registry_service:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-ACCOUNTING-REGISTRY-009
      status: AGREED
      text: 'Reprint responsibility and customer billing consequence are separate fields: an internal-defect reprint
        may consume material/cost without a new customer charge.'
      context: Accounting records the financial consequence; OCR owns the reprint execution workflow.
      roadmap: ACC-REPRINT-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-ACCOUNTING-REGISTRY-010
      status: AGREED
      text: Proof/sample purpose does not automatically mean free; billing policy can be FREE, INCLUDED or PAID
        per case/policy.
      context: Do not infer finance solely from the legacy proba token.
      roadmap: ACC-PROOF-01
  warehouse_service:
    - intent_id: HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-007
      status: AGREED
      text: A production reprint caused by internal defect must generate traceable additional physical material
        consumption/writeoff without silently changing the original planned quantity.
      context: OCR provides reprint execution context; Warehouse remains physical stock truth.
      roadmap: WH-REPRINT-01
    - intent_id: HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-008
      status: AGREED
      text: Legacy filename material tokens are parser candidates; Warehouse consumes canonical material IDs after
        Library/Calculator normalization.
      context: Do not make warehouse stock truth depend on a misspelled filename token.
      roadmap: WH-FILE-01
```

```yaml
schema_version: forprint_human_intent_delta_v0_1
date: '2026-09-01'
status: PLANNING_INTEGRATION_NOT_RELEASE_AUTHORITY
authority: operator_evening_discussion_2026_08_31_to_2026_09_01
modules:
  forprint_system_blueprint:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-019
      status: AGREED
      text: Important operator wording that explains why a roadmap step exists must be preserved as curated verbatim
        quotes linked to Human Intent and roadmap context.
      context: Preserve selected exact wording, not a full chat archive.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-05
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-020
      status: AGREED
      text: Every module roadmap must show a visible route to a mature Final Target State split into human-confirmed
        and synthetic/proposed capabilities.
      context: If a useful mature purpose cannot be formulated even synthetically, reassess whether the module should
        exist.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-06
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-021
      status: AGREED
      text: Ordinary module implementation remains held until full-horizon roadmaps, module self-inventory and a
        dependency/readiness/priority dashboard are sufficiently complete.
      context: Planning, inventory and Blueprint tooling remain allowed.
      roadmap: BP-PORTFOLIO-07
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-022
      status: AGREED
      text: Meaningful new capabilities must be checked against project capability/index surfaces before implementation
        to reduce duplicate code and semantic drift.
      context: Self-inventory is an architecture control, not only documentation.
      roadmap: BP-SEMANTIC-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-023
      status: AGREED
      text: ForPrint uses one central PostgreSQL operational platform with logical domain schemas, stable shared
        IDs, controlled writes and reporting projections.
      context: Physical centralization does not mean shared semantic ownership.
      roadmap: BP-DATA-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-024
      status: AGREED
      text: ForPrint Control Center uses left-side module navigation and top tabs within the selected module workspace.
      context: Cloud Backup Manager is a reference shell; shared Library UI rules remain authoritative.
      roadmap: BP-UI-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-025
      status: AGREED
      text: Deterministic-to-AI fallbacks should be logged and periodically reviewed for conversion into safe deterministic
        automation.
      context: Do not repeatedly pay for the same solvable automation gap.
      roadmap: BP-AUTOMATION-01
  calculator_engine:
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-022
      status: AGREED
      text: 'Exact Calculator references are recovered: sborka.ua is primary; FastPrint Pro and Wolf calc-polygraphy
        are secondary.'
      context: Comparative reference only; not canonical truth or mandatory clone.
      roadmap: CALC-REF-01
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-023
      status: AGREED
      text: Exact constructor references include Vizitka universal creator, FATLINE constructor and Maikoff constructor.
      context: Use them to study reusable personalized-product constructor patterns.
      roadmap: CALC-REF-02
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-024
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator needs an early visual product/configuration interface so operator usability feedback arrives
        while rules are still evolving.
      context: Visual UI is an early development instrument, not end-stage cosmetics.
      roadmap: CALC-UI-01
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-025
      status: AGREED
      text: Calculator should support structured outsourcing of jobs to approved print partners when internal capacity/equipment/staff
        cannot safely fulfill them.
      context: Partner-specific execution belongs behind provider adapters.
      roadmap: CALC-OUTSOURCE-01
    - intent_id: HI-CALCULATOR-ENGINE-026
      status: AGREED
      text: If a partner has no usable API, controlled browser automation may be used with evidence, retry bounds
        and human escalation.
      context: Credentials remain in centralized secrets infrastructure.
      roadmap: CALC-OUTSOURCE-02
  forprint_crm:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-013
      status: AGREED
      text: 'CRM is a unified operational entry point: one guided interface may compose related actions across multiple
        owner modules.'
      context: Humans should not manually visit many module tabs for one normal workflow.
      roadmap: CRM-WORKSPACE-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-014
      status: AGREED
      text: CRM should provide configurable role/department dashboards and large-screen wallboards for operational
        visibility.
      context: Keep dashboard capability encapsulated in CRM for now and extract later only if objectively justified.
      roadmap: CRM-DASH-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-015
      status: AGREED
      text: CRM may read/present cross-domain truth and submit structured commands, but it does not own Accounting,
        Warehouse, Production, Logistics or other foreign domain truth.
      context: Shared PostgreSQL does not remove logical ownership.
      roadmap: CRM-BOUNDARY-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-016
      status: AGREED
      text: Management/CRM views should surface plan-versus-actual variance for time, material, cost/price, reprints,
        delays and resource blockers.
      context: Configurable tolerance breaches become visible exceptions.
      roadmap: CRM-DASH-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-017
      status: AGREED
      text: A composite CRM workflow should support onboarding a new supplier/material from partial information
        and route enrichment/validation to domain owners.
      context: Enter known information once; gate only critical missing facts before irreversible actions.
      roadmap: CRM-WORKSPACE-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-CRM-018
      status: AGREED
      text: CRM should evolve toward Customer/Supplier 360, universal search, cross-module activity timeline, saved
        role workspaces and exception/task centers.
      context: These are views/projections, not a competing master database.
      roadmap: CRM-WORKSPACE-03
  forprint_accounting_registry_service:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-ACCOUNTING-REGISTRY-011
      status: AGREED
      text: Accounting should support incoming supplier-document parsing for Excel, PDF, Word and scans/photos with
        human confirmation when financial facts are uncertain.
      context: Repeated supplier templates should become increasingly deterministic.
      roadmap: ACC-GOODS-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-ACCOUNTING-REGISTRY-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Supplier-specific descriptions/part numbers map to one canonical material while preserving supplier
        alias/SKU provenance.
      context: The organization's preferred material identity remains stable.
      roadmap: ACC-GOODS-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-ACCOUNTING-REGISTRY-013
      status: AGREED
      text: A mature Accounting roadmap may include bounded preauthorized conditional payment mandates with strict
        limits, idempotency and audit.
      context: Early stages require confirmation; auto-payment is later high-risk automation.
      roadmap: ACC-PAYMENT-01
  forprint_operations_control_registry:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-018
      status: AGREED
      text: Stable shared Business Partner/person/organization and order/job identifiers should link Calculator,
        Accounting, Warehouse, Production, Logistics and CRM records.
      context: Operations Control Registry remains the current operational write/semantic boundary for shared party/order
        state.
      roadmap: OCR-DATA-01
  forprint_library:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-014
      status: AGREED
      text: Library is publication owner for shared ForPrint UI tokens, themes, reusable components, component catalog,
        versions and adoption metadata.
      context: Blueprint governs; consumers compose; Inspector validates.
      roadmap: LIB-UI-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-LIBRARY-015
      status: AGREED
      text: Shared indexes should help modules discover reusable technical primitives and semantic definitions before
        creating competing implementations.
      context: Domain business rules still belong to domain owners.
      roadmap: LIB-INDEX-01
  forprint_system_administration:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-SYSTEM-ADMINISTRATION-009
      status: AGREED
      text: System Administration owns the physical PostgreSQL platform operations and centralized secrets infrastructure.
      context: Domain modules retain semantic ownership; provider credentials do not live in Telegram/Calculator.
      roadmap: SYS-DATA-01
  forprint_project_inspector:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-019
      status: AGREED
      text: Inspector should detect shared-UI drift, stale component adoption, self-inventory inconsistency, duplicate
        capability implementations and cross-module semantic divergence.
      context: Inspector reports/routes; it does not own the underlying semantics.
      roadmap: PI-S11
  telegram_bot:
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-012
      status: AGREED
      text: Telegram should support bounded supplier/contact enrichment dialogues that collect missing structured
        information without repeated manual relaying.
      context: Confirmed facts are routed to the correct domain owner.
      roadmap: TG-SUPPLIER-01
    - intent_id: HI-TELEGRAM-BOT-013
      status: AGREED
      text: Telegram roadmap should include voice-message transcription feeding the same structured intent pipeline
        as text, with future TTS/voice interaction later.
      context: Voice is a channel modality, not separate business logic.
      roadmap: TG-VOICE-01
  forprint_identity_access_service:
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-001
      status: AGREED
      text: ForPrint should use one shared Identity & Access Service instead of separate authentication inside Website,
        Calculator, CRM and Mobile.
      context: The module existed as a prior candidate and is now explicitly confirmed for formation.
      roadmap: IAM-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-002
      status: AGREED
      text: Identity, authentication and authorization are distinct concerns and must remain explicit in architecture.
      context: Business relationship truth remains outside credential/session ownership.
      roadmap: IAM-01
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-003
      status: AGREED
      text: Access control must support role defaults plus explicit per-user permission additions or restrictions.
      context: A manager can receive extra Production/Logistics access without a duplicate person.
      roadmap: IAM-03
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-004
      status: AGREED
      text: Phone, email and Telegram are identifiers/contact paths, not immutable primary database keys.
      context: Use stable internal Account/Person IDs.
      roadmap: IAM-02
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-005
      status: AGREED
      text: Cross-client/cross-organization access is deny-by-default and requires explicit scoped permission.
      context: Representing multiple organizations does not grant universal access.
      roadmap: IAM-04
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-006
      status: PROPOSED
      text: Mature Identity should support sessions/devices, recovery, revocation, MFA/passkeys and auditable security
        events.
      context: Synthetic full-horizon capability for owner review.
      roadmap: IAM-05
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-007
      status: PROPOSED
      text: Mature Identity should provide shared SSO/session semantics across ForPrint web/internal/mobile surfaces.
      context: Exact protocols remain an implementation decision.
      roadmap: IAM-07
    - intent_id: HI-FP-IDENTITY-ACCESS-008
      status: AGREED
      text: External provider passwords/API keys are not user identity and belong to centralized secrets infrastructure.
      context: Identity may authorize access to secret-backed actions but does not own the external secret.
      roadmap: IAM-BOUNDARY-01
```

```md
- Intent: Після кожного великого вечірнього діалогу має з’являтися не лише технічний пакет інтеграції, а й окремий людський список домовленостей по кожному модулю.

- Intent: Project cleanliness must be a permanent project-wide policy: Blueprint keeps the single canonical cleanliness rules, every module is responsible for cleanliness of its own repository, and modules must not create competing global standards or uncontrolled document/surface variants.
- Context: Operator explicitly requires the cleanup procedure learned in Blueprint to become a general rule for all modules.
- Roadmap: `BP-PORTFOLIO-08`

### HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-030

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Automated cleanliness capability must be discoverable and status-explicit: existing Blueprint checks are recorded as implemented; missing module-local and cross-repository controls are recorded in portfolio roadmaps before future execution.
- Context: Do not describe planned automation as already implemented; keep implemented evidence and future work separate.
- Roadmap: `BP-PORTFOLIO-09`

### HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-031

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector is the cross-repository cleanliness/conformance auditor, while each module retains local semantic ownership and local repository cleanliness responsibility.
- Context: Inspector detects/report drift and duplicates but must not silently rewrite domain truth.
- Roadmap: `BP-PORTFOLIO-09`

### HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-032

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Before future module assistants are started, Blueprint must define and review an AI Execution Safety and Runtime Governance gate covering ingress validation, tool-call validation, egress/data-release control, bounded retries/loops, cost budgets, execution logging and manual escalation.
- Context: A raw Telegram message or arbitrary AI request must not automatically become trusted executable work.
- Roadmap: `BP-AI-SAFETY-01`

### HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-033

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Retry, clarification, inter-module, database, tool, AI/token/cost and time limits must be configurable by operation/risk class rather than one universal fixed retry count.
- Context: Repeated identical invalid calls must terminate or escalate instead of entering an unbounded loop.
- Roadmap: `BP-AI-SAFETY-02`

### HI-FP-SYSTEM-BLUEPRINT-034

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Before the next detailed portfolio is approved, older and under-documented modules must be reconciled against their repository inventories so historical capabilities, stale instructions and misowned functionality are not silently carried into the mature architecture.
- Context: Calculator and Telegram are especially important because they existed before most current domain modules.
- Roadmap: `BP-PORTFOLIO-10`

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: The mature ecosystem should reduce routine human steering to a small number of control surfaces while modules operate under bounded, reversible managed autonomy.

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-013

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: AI-generated commands are not authorization: side effects must pass a deterministic Execution Policy Gate, IAM/resource policy and capability-shaped tool boundary.

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-014

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Development, Verification, Release and Runtime are separate execution profiles; runtime deployed code must not self-modify live source or install arbitrary dependencies.

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-015

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Canonical module maturity should follow M0 portfolio justification → M1 governance/roadmap → M2 development baseline → M3 capability implementation → M4 verification readiness → M5 adversarial/resilience verification → M6 release candidate → M7 staging/shadow/canary → M8 production → M9 managed self-evolution.

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-001

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Logistics Service is the reference pilot for reusable module self-knowledge, fresh-worker, coordination and reporting behavior; proven patterns should later be propagated to other canonical modules.
- Context: Modules inherit the behavioral contract, not necessarily Logistics domain internals or byte-for-byte code.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-REFERENCE-ROLLOUT`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: The first substantial Logistics execution pool is fully manually gated; every prompt result is reviewed by the operator before the next prompt is released.
- Context: The first pool calibrates quality, continuity and reporting before any automation widening.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-POOL-CONTROL`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Later pools may use full-manual, checkpointed or automated child progression depending on prior-pool review, but every pool always ends at a human review gate.
- Context: Automation level is selected by the operator and never widens itself.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-POOL-CONTROL`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Machine closure of a child prompt inside an approved pool is conformance/progression evidence and must not be silently redefined as Blueprint ACCEPT.
- Context: Preserve explicit human ACCEPT/RETURN/HOLD semantics while enabling future intra-pool automation.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-POOL-CONTROL`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: All modules must report prompt completion through one normalized semantic envelope so Blueprint and Inspector can validate them with shared tooling rather than interpreting different prose formats.
- Context: Module internals may differ; completion evidence shape and semantics should be standardized.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-UNIFIED-REPORTING`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: The target execution model starts a fresh AI worker for each prompt and reconstructs continuity from repository-owned AGENTS, inventory/index, current authority, current prompt/contract/oracle and previous completion evidence.
- Context: Conversation memory must not be required for safe continuation.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-FRESH-WORKER`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: After the first Logistics pool, perform an independent inventory/audit and compare worker claims against repository reality before selecting the next automation level or propagating the reference profile.
- Context: Self-reporting alone is insufficient to decide whether automation is mature.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-REFERENCE-ROLLOUT`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Temporary assistant/operator artifacts must have semantic unique non-overwriting names; cleanup permission is optional and the operator may retain archives for later analysis.
- Context: Artifact identity should survive relocation outside its original tmp session folder.
- Roadmap: `BP-ASSISTANT-HANDOFF`

### HI-FP-U129B-20260905-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: In Ukrainian the AI assistant uses feminine grammatical self-reference and addresses the human operator with masculine grammatical forms.
- Context: Make the convention explicit in AGENTS-linked documentation so it is not repeatedly clarified.
- Roadmap: `BP-ASSISTANT-INTERACTION`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-001

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Module inventory is intended as long-lived implementation memory: it must explain purpose, provenance, lifecycle, lineage, interfaces, dependencies, evidence, duplicates, roadmap role and retirement impact rather than acting as a file list.
- Context: The current Inventory Program is the foundation and should be extended rather than replaced.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-INVENTORY-VNEXT`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Blueprint Control Plane evolution runs in parallel with the Logistics pilot and must reuse existing transparency, repository-knowledge freshness, safe-mutation and context-bundle primitives instead of creating competing frameworks.
- Context: The Logistics pilot must not be blocked by broad control-plane modernization unless a concrete safety dependency exists.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-CONTROL-PLANE`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Critical current-state projections need explicit freshness and dependency contracts so a source change can deterministically identify which AGENTS, inventory, lineage, index, roadmap or context surfaces became stale.
- Context: Fresh workers should start from a coherent snapshot with freshness proof, not from documents merely assumed current.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-FRESHNESS`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Significant implementation surfaces should eventually carry a short language-independent machine-readable metadata passport that identifies semantic role and points to durable inventory/lineage without embedding a full historical log in source code.
- Context: Trivial helpers and generated/support surfaces should not create global metadata bureaucracy.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-IMPLEMENTATION-METADATA`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: ForPrint should converge gradually on stable shared semantic IDs, aliases, contracts, events and statuses without a mass rename; old implementations may be mapped or migrated on touch while shared semantics become increasingly standardized.
- Context: Semantic identity, implementation name and contract version are separate concepts.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-SEMANTIC-CONVERGENCE`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Before creating a significant potentially shared implementation, workers should query the existing Library semantic reference; missing or insufficient semantics should use registration/enrichment proposals and explicit provisional bindings without blocking ordinary local work.
- Context: Cross-module breaking or high-risk interfaces may still require approval before integration.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-SEMANTIC-CONVERGENCE`

### HI-FP-U131-20260906-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector should use Library semantics, module inventory, implementation metadata and dependency relationships to filter candidates before deep source analysis and report whether matches are canonical, alias-based, provisional or inferred.
- Context: The objective is to reduce AI guessing and focus human attention on ambiguous inferred cases.
- Roadmap: `BP-H10-INSPECTOR-CONFIDENCE`

# ForPrint Operations Assistant

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-001

- Intent: Operations Assistant має бути максимально простим shop-floor interface: телефон/планшет, QR, великі кнопки, мінімум тексту, poka-yoke.
- Context: Працівник не повинен вчитися працювати зі складною ERP.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Працівник є сенсором: швидко повідомляє факт/аномалію, а система валідовує і маршрутизує його до canonical owner.
- Context: Assistant не стає Accounting/Warehouse/production truth.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-003

- Status: `RECOVERED`
- Intent: Потрібні guided forms і contextual knowledge для receiving, stock check, production checkpoints, QC та видачі.
- Context: Це вже було в старому портфоліо.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Для оцінки кількості аркушів або дрібних однотипних предметів має бути практичний режим: поміряти лінійкою висоту стопки і оцінити кількість по calibration profile.
- Context: Саме цей 'лінійка + висота стопки' контекст треба зберігати як людську тезу, а не стискати до абстрактного quantity estimation.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-005

- Status: `AGREED`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Якщо вимірювання виходить за нормальний діапазон, Assistant має запропонувати next safe action: переміряти, розбити стопку, запросити керівника, перейти на вагу/поштучний контроль.
- Context: Не давати впевнене число при поганій якості вимірювання.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-007

- Status: `AGREED`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-008

- Status: `AGREED`

- Context: Перевага: текст -> картинки/схеми -> анімація -> реальне відео.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Assistant повинен показувати STOP/hold події вище звичайної черги і вимагати acknowledgement, якщо працівник є адресатом критичної події.
- Context: Скасоване замовлення не повинно тихо залишатися в списку робіт.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-010

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Усі physical observations повинні мати evidence там, де це практично: фото, timestamp, device/operator, quantity/measurement context.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Лінійка/вага/камера/QR — це різні sensing methods; система повинна обирати найпростіший достатньо точний метод під конкретний матеріал.
- Context: Не кожну інвентаризацію треба робити однаково.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-012

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Operations Assistant should teach a new worker how to read the short production filename and which material/process each token implies in the active profile.
- Context: Use text first, then schemes/reference images, later real equipment photos when useful.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-013

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Operations Assistant displays the current Job Ticket on phone/tablet and can request another paper copy if the original is lost or damaged.
- Context: OCR owns the ticket revision; Assistant is the human interface.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-014

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Assistant production queue should expose structured priority and HOLD state with text/icon as well as color; color alone is insufficient.
- Context: Urgent jobs must be obvious and held jobs must not look runnable.
- Roadmap: `OA-QUEUE-01`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-015

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-016

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Assistant should expose a simple reprint reporting action from the Job Ticket/QR flow so the worker can report defect/reprint reason without learning a complex ERP.
- Context: The resulting reprint semantics belong to OCR/Accounting/Warehouse.

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-017

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Operations Assistant must provide equipment error/help lookup from parsed and indexed approved equipment documentation, including Ukrainian explanation and source illustrations when present.
- Context: Exact error-code lookup should be preferred when a code exists.
- Roadmap: `OPS-EQUIP-01`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-018

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: For production equipment, Operations Assistant must not invent repair procedures or freely search the internet for a repair answer; if approved documentation contains no answer it returns NOT_FOUND and escalates.
- Context: Production-equipment troubleshooting is documentation-grounded only.
- Roadmap: `OPS-EQUIP-02`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-019

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Equipment-problem escalation is role-aware: a normal printer/operator can escalate to the production manager, while an authorized manager may create a service-support request through an approved channel.
- Context: Permissions control who may contact the external service directly.
- Roadmap: `OPS-EQUIP-03`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-ASSISTANT-020

- Status: `PROPOSED`
- Intent: Indexed manual procedures should progress from text and static figures to ordered step galleries and only later to optional animation where it adds real value.
- Context: Animation is a later usability enhancement, not an early dependency.
- Roadmap: `OPS-EQUIP-04`

### HI-FP-OPERATIONS-CONTROL-REGISTRY-001

# ForPrint CRM

### HI-FP-CRM-001

- Context: Телефон, email, Telegram/Viber IDs — це identifiers, а не сама сутність людини.

### HI-FP-CRM-007

- Status: `RECOVERED`

- Context: Вона не повинна ставати другою базою клієнтів/замовлень/платежів.

### HI-FP-CRM-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: CRM бере контактний контекст для AR collection, але сам фінансовий стан боргу належить Accounting.

### HI-FP-CRM-012

### HI-FP-CRM-014

### HI-FP-CRM-016

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Management/CRM views should surface plan-versus-actual variance for time, material, cost/price, reprints, delays and resource blockers.
- Context: Configurable tolerance breaches become visible exceptions.
- Roadmap: `CRM-DASH-02`

### HI-FP-CRM-017

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: A composite CRM workflow should support onboarding a new supplier/material from partial information and route enrichment/validation to domain owners.

### HI-FP-CRM-018

- Status: `AGREED`

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-002

# ForPrint Semantic Retrieval Service (PROPOSED)

Module ID: `forprint_semantic_retrieval_service`
Ledger: `coordination/human_intent/modules/forprint_semantic_retrieval_service.yaml`
Intent count: **12**

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-001

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Ключовий принцип Semantic Retrieval: search finds candidates, domain owner decides truth.
- Context: Пошук не може сам створити canonical клієнта, матеріал чи замовлення.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Сервіс має бути cross-cutting: exact IDs + structured filters + lexical/BM25/trigram + text embeddings + image embeddings + domain/ACL filters + reranking.
- Context: Гібридний пошук, а не лише vector DB.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Search projection містить entity_id, entity_type, source_module, searchable_text, structured attrs, embedding/media refs, source_revision, indexed_at.
- Context: Truth лишається у CRM/Warehouse/Library/OCR.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Result state має бути явним: MATCHES / UNCERTAIN / NO_MATCH / POSSIBLE_NEW_ENTITY.
- Context: Краще UNCERTAIN, ніж неправильний confident match.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Warehouse use case: якщо QR зіпсований або відсутній, працівник описує предмет або фотографує його, а система показує кандидатів.
- Context: Вибір остаточно підтверджує доменний owner/оператор.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Telegram use case: клієнт надсилає фото і пише 'мені треба така штука'; Semantic Retrieval повертає candidates, Telegram уточнює і передає підтверджений контекст у Calculator.
- Context: Це один із головних multimodal сценаріїв.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Потрібен evaluation corpus: recall@5, precision, MRR, no-match accuracy, false-confident-match rate.
- Context: Без вимірювання semantic search легко здається кращим, ніж є.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: ACL filtering має відбуватися до disclosure результатів.
- Context: Навіть релевантний кандидат не можна показати користувачу без права доступу.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Сервіс поки лишається PROPOSED_FOR_FORMAL_ADOPTION, не активний canonical module.
- Context: Спочатку formal adoption у Blueprint окремим кроком.

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-010

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic Retrieval remains a noncanonical review-only candidate until its independent module value is proven.
- Context: Do not promote it automatically because earlier artifacts exist.
- Roadmap: `SEM-REVIEW-01`

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic retrieval may find fuzzy/semantic candidates such as a likely material or related document, but the relevant domain owner remains responsible for canonical truth and current state.
- Context: Search finds candidates; domain owner decides truth.
- Roadmap: `SEM-BOUNDARY-01`

### HI-FP-SEMANTIC-RETRIEVAL-SERVICE-012

- Status: `PROPOSED`
- Intent: Candidate value should be evaluated for multilingual/fuzzy material search and Project Inspector document/procedure similarity discovery before deciding PROMOTE, MERGE or RETIRE.
- Context: Evaluation must compare the service with simpler alias/full-text/search approaches.
- Roadmap: `SEM-EVAL-01`

- Intent: Library is publication owner for shared ForPrint UI tokens, themes, reusable components, component catalog, versions and adoption metadata.

### HI-FP-LIBRARY-015

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Shared indexes should help modules discover reusable technical primitives and semantic definitions before creating competing implementations.

### HI-LIBRARY-U131-20260906-001

### HI-LIBRARY-U131-20260906-002

- Intent: A first registered semantic ID may remain stable while maturity, recommended implementation names, aliases and contract versions evolve.

### HI-LIBRARY-U131-20260906-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic registration should prioritize module-public, cross-module, external-boundary, high-criticality and widely reused surfaces rather than every local helper.
- Context: Interaction scope and criticality are independent signals for standardization review.
- Roadmap: `LIB-SEMANTIC-REGISTRY`

### HI-LIBRARY-U131-20260906-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Library should batch unresolved semantic registration/enrichment proposals and publish versioned semantic changes; Blueprint remains responsible for rollout timing and adoption mode across affected modules.
- Context: Adoption may be informational, new-code-only, migrate-on-touch, roadmap-required or migration-required.

### HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-008

### HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-009

- Status: `AGREED`

### HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-010

- Status: `AGREED`

### HI-WAREHOUSE-SERVICE-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Warehouse must support controlled write-off/adjustment reasons for damaged, spoiled or otherwise unusable materials while preserving traceability of the stock movement.
- Context: Storage damage and production-related material loss must not disappear from stock without evidence.
- Roadmap: `WH-ADJUST-01`

# Production Runtime Inspector

Module ID: `production_runtime_inspector`
Ledger: `coordination/human_intent/modules/production_runtime_inspector.yaml`
Intent count: **12**

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-001

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Production Runtime Inspector збирає machine and runtime telemetry, але не визначає бізнес-статус замовлення.
- Context: Він є technical evidence source.

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Telemetry потрібна для actual cycle time, downtime, machine problems і виявлення аномалій.
- Context: Operations Control Registry використовує її для planned-vs-actual view.

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Broken machine або довгі простої не повинні автоматично 'навчити' систему, що погана швидкість є новою нормою.
- Context: Norm change лише через proposal/review.

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-004

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime data треба корелювати з order_id/operation_id/machine_id/revision там, де це безпечно.
- Context: Щоб факти не змішувалися між роботами.

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: При STOP_WORK critical state runtime integration не повинна запускати нові commands після gate check failure.
- Context: Перед machine command перевіряється current order state.

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime Inspector records the concrete physical device instance actually used for an operation, not only a generic machine family.
- Context: Needed for maintenance, quality and true machine-performance analytics.
- Roadmap: `PRI-DEVICE-01`

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Similar/replaceable printers may share a capability queue while runtime evidence still identifies the exact device used.
- Context: Planning capability and actual telemetry are separate concerns.
- Roadmap: `PRI-DEVICE-02`

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime Inspector must observe end-to-end execution traces: process start, module/tool calls, waits, durations, retries, outcomes and dependency blocking.
- Context: Its focus is what the live system actually executed, not repository/project conformance.
- Roadmap: `RTI-TRACE-01`

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime Inspector must expose repeated-call, retry, wait-time and bottleneck evidence so loops and asymmetric producer/consumer delays can be detected and analyzed.
- Context: Example: one module repeatedly waiting on another should become visible as runtime evidence.
- Roadmap: `RTI-LOOP-01`

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-010

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime governance must track separate configurable budgets for retries, repeated call signatures, inter-module hops, database/tool calls, AI usage/cost and timeout/TTL.
- Context: Configured budget exhaustion must lead to stop/degraded/manual-review behavior rather than endless retries.
- Roadmap: `RTI-BUDGET-01`

### HI-PRODUCTION-RUNTIME-INSPECTOR-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Runtime observability should minimize sensitive payload exposure; identifiers, contract versions, status, timings, classifications/hashes and error evidence are preferred unless payload access is explicitly authorized.
- Context: Observability must not create a new unrestricted data-leak surface.
- Roadmap: `RTI-DATA-01`

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-010

- Status: `PROPOSED`
- Intent: Runtime evidence should include AI/tool calls, repeat patterns, tokens, latency, cost, retries, escalation and budget-runway signals; provisional context checkpoint/handoff thresholds remain policy-tunable rather than hard business truth.

# ForPrint Project Inspector

Module ID: `forprint_project_inspector`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-001

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector має незалежно перевіряти completion attestation проти pinned governance snapshot.

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-004

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Safe deterministic repairs можуть з’явитися пізніше, але semantic/business changes не повинні виконуватись Inspector-ом самостійно.
- Context: Межа автоматизації має бути явною.

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-006

- Status: `AGREED`

- Context: Не робити фальшиві FAIL лише через новішу ревізію.

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector owns Semantic Consistency Audit as an internal capability; no separate semantic-audit ForPrint module is created.
- Context: Keep the ecosystem small; Inspector remains independent and does not become domain owner.
- Roadmap: `PI-S1`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Incremental Inspector review must compare last_inspected_commit/checkpoint to the current target commit, not rely only on git status.
- Context: Committed daytime changes must remain visible to the next audit.
- Roadmap: `PI-S1`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Anything that can be proven deterministically should be checked by scripts before involving an LLM.
- Context: Schemas, revisions, indexes, tests, contract support and evidence are cheaper/more reliable mechanically.
- Roadmap: `PI-S2`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-010

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: When interpretive policy/business/legal/customer-facing content changes, Inspector builds a bounded semantic audit package for an LLM worker.
- Context: The worker receives changed content plus applicable authorities, contracts, Human Intent and explicit questions.
- Roadmap: `PI-S4`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: The semantic LLM worker may ask Inspector for additional bounded context instead of guessing missing authority.
- Context: Inspector can repack relevant documents and rerun the review.
- Roadmap: `PI-S5`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-012

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic review triggers are risk-based, not diff-size-based; a one-line policy change can require immediate LLM review.
- Context: High-risk categories include identity, payment, legal, customer-facing, production-safety and authority changes.
- Roadmap: `PI-S7`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-013

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: A portfolio-wide semantic sweep should initially run about monthly and may move to quarterly/semiannual cadence as the architecture matures.
- Context: Event-driven high-risk audits remain active even when full-sweep cadence decreases.
- Roadmap: `PI-S8`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-014

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector semantic findings need a stable taxonomy covering contradiction, ambiguity, ownership violation, contract divergence, term drift, stale authority, Human Intent divergence, hidden defaults, orphan rules and cross-module behavior conflict.
- Context: Findings must be structured rather than a vague AI opinion.
- Roadmap: `PI-S6`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-015

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic findings must carry severity, confidence, evidence, affected modules and recommended resolution owner.
- Context: The report must be usable by Blueprint/human governance.
- Roadmap: `PI-S6`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-016

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector and its LLM worker detect, verify, compare, classify, report and route; they do not invent business rules or silently rewrite domain semantics.
- Context: A suspected defect in an authority document is routed back to that authority owner.
- Roadmap: `PI-S10`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-017

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: For legally meaningful content, LLM review performs issue spotting and contradiction analysis but does not declare material legal compliance confirmed.
- Context: Final legal certainty remains a separate human/legal-review boundary.
- Roadmap: `PI-S10`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-018

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector should consume a future machine-readable Cross-Module Invariant Registry maintained under Blueprint governance.
- Context: The registry makes semantic checks more deterministic without giving Inspector semantic ownership.
- Roadmap: `PI-S3`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-019

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector should detect shared-UI drift, stale component adoption, self-inventory inconsistency, duplicate capability implementations and cross-module semantic divergence.
- Context: Inspector reports/routes; it does not own the underlying semantics.
- Roadmap: `PI-S11`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-020

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector must audit repository cleanliness in Blueprint and other ForPrint repositories, including schema drift, generated-file drift, duplicate capability/semantic surfaces, orphan current documents, naming/folder deviations and uncontrolled temporary artifacts.
- Context: Inspector detects and routes evidence; it does not become semantic owner of another module.
- Roadmap: `PI-CLEAN-01`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-021

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector must detect candidate duplicate/equivalent functions and compare them with common fixtures, edge cases and invalid inputs to expose semantic divergence such as different price/rounding results.
- Context: A candidate duplicate is not automatically the same semantic function; evidence is required.
- Roadmap: `PI-DUP-01`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-022

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: When equivalent functionality exists in multiple modules, Inspector should report implementation quality, ownership and consolidation options but must not automatically merge or replace implementations.
- Context: Target direction is one appropriate canonical owner/contract where semantics are truly shared.
- Roadmap: `PI-DUP-02`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-023

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Dormant scripts, functions, and documents are review candidates, not automatic deletion candidates; Inspector must use evidence such as usage, ownership, dependencies, documentation, fallback purpose and current roadmap relevance.
- Context: Rare disaster-recovery or safety functionality can be important despite long periods without use.
- Roadmap: `PI-DORMANT-01`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-024

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector must identify orphaned or misowned historical capabilities and route them for KEEP/MOVE/MERGE/REWRITE/DEPRECATE/REMOVE review with evidence.
- Context: Old Calculator/Telegram functionality is a priority area for this audit.
- Roadmap: `PI-OWNERSHIP-01`

### HI-FP-PROJECT-INSPECTOR-025

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Project Inspector must support periodic and risk-triggered conformance audits and emit bounded findings with evidence, severity, responsible owner and recommended disposition.
- Context: Cleanliness, duplicate standards, stale instructions and semantic conflicts are ongoing controls.
- Roadmap: `PI-AUDIT-01`

### HI-INSPECTOR-U131-20260906-001

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector should resolve a question to shared semantics, filter modules through inventory/index evidence, then filter significant implementations through metadata and dependency relationships before deep source inspection.
- Context: This reduces full-repository semantic guessing.
- Roadmap: `INSPECTOR-SEMANTIC-QUERY`

### HI-INSPECTOR-U131-20260906-002

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector findings should distinguish exact canonical matches, known aliases, linked provisional implementations and AI-inferred matches so uncertainty is visible rather than hidden.
- Context: Manual review should concentrate on inferred or conflicting cases.
- Roadmap: `INSPECTOR-SEMANTIC-CONFIDENCE`

### HI-INSPECTOR-U131-20260906-003

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Inspector should detect duplicate current implementations without lineage/coexistence justification and later support read-only impact and conformance queries over shared contracts.
- Context: Inspector advises and verifies; it must not silently delete, merge or reassign semantic ownership.
- Roadmap: `INSPECTOR-INVENTORY-CONFORMANCE`

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-004

- Status: `RECOVERED`

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-005

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Semantic Retrieval є окремим сервісом і не повинен перетворювати Gateway на search/AI engine.

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Gateway should validate and route runtime payloads against published ACTIVE contract revisions rather than owning the contract lifecycle itself.
- Context: Contract Registry says what is agreed; Gateway applies that agreement in transport/runtime.

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-007

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Gateway must not silently reinterpret Library/CRM/Accounting semantics when normalizing a payload.
- Context: If a semantic mapping changes, it requires the appropriate authority/contract revision.

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-008

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Integration Gateway must provide a canonical inter-module message envelope and validate contract/schema/version compatibility before routing a request or response.
- Context: Internal module implementation can differ; the boundary format must remain controlled.
- Roadmap: `IG-CONTRACT-01`

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-009

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Invalid or incompatible payloads must be rejected/quarantined with visible evidence rather than silently guessed, reshaped or ignored.
- Context: The operator needs an administrative view of invalid exchanges and why they failed.
- Roadmap: `IG-VALIDATE-01`

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-010

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Gateway integration control must include idempotency, bounded retries, dead-letter/quarantine behavior and audit/correlation evidence for failed or repeated inter-module exchanges.
- Context: Transport reliability must not create duplicate business operations or hidden infinite loops.
- Roadmap: `IG-RELIABILITY-01`

### HI-FP-INTEGRATION-GATEWAY-011

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Gateway validates transport/contract boundaries but does not become universal business-semantic owner; domain modules validate their own semantics and Contract Registry controls contract lifecycle.
- Context: Runtime Inspector observes execution; Project Inspector audits design/conformance.
- Roadmap: `IG-BOUNDARY-01`

### HI-FP-U92-20260903-006

- Status: `AGREED`
- Intent: Shared resource concurrency must preserve persistent request state and explicit queue/wait/retry/degraded outcomes; TEMPORARILY_UNAVAILABLE is not equivalent to NOT_FOUND.
```
